Pass empty strings for default URLs.\nfunc New(registryURL, downloadsURL string) *Provider {\n\tif registryURL == \"\" {\n\t\tregistryURL = defaultRegistryURL\n\t}\n\tif downloadsURL == \"\" {\n\t\tdownloadsURL = defaultDownloadsURL\n\t}\n\treturn &Provider{\n\t\tregistryURL:  strings.TrimRight(registryURL, \"/\"),\n\t\tdownloadsURL: strings.TrimRight(downloadsURL, \"/\"),\n\t\tclient:       &http.Client{},\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (p *Provider) Scheme() string { return \"npm\" }\n\nfunc (p *Provider) Fetch(ctx context.Context, identifier string) (provider.Result, error) {\n\tif identifier == \"\" {\n\t\treturn provider.Result{\n\t\t\tTarget: \"npm:\",\n\t\t\tError:  \"invalid identifier: package name must not be empty\",\n\t\t}, nil\n\t}\n\n\tmetrics := &provider.NPMMetrics{}\n\tvar mu sync.Mutex\n\tvar wg sync.WaitGroup\n\tvar errs []string\n\n\ttype job struct {\n\t\tname string\n\t\tfn   func(context.Context) error\n\t}\n\n\tjobs := []job{\n\t\t{\"latest\", func(ctx context.Context) error {\n\t\t\tlatest, err := p.fetchLatest(ctx, identifier)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmu.Lock()\n\t\t\tmetrics.LatestVersion = latest.Version\n\t\t\tmetrics.DependenciesCount = len(latest.Dependencies)\n\t\t\tmetrics.License = latest.License\n\t\t\tmu.Unlock()\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t}},\n\t\t{\"modified\", func(ctx context.Context) error {\n\t\t\tdays, err := p.fetchLastPublishDays(ctx, identifier)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmu.Lock()\n\t\t\tmetrics.LastPublishDays = days\n\t\t\tmu.Unlock()\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t}},\n\t\t{\"downloads\", func(ctx context.Context) error {\n\t\t\tcount, err := p.fetchWeeklyDownloads(ctx, identifier)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmu.Lock()\n\t\t\tmetrics.WeeklyDownloads = count\n\t\t\tmu.Unlock()\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t}},\n\t}\n\n\twg.Add(len(jobs))\n\tfor _, j := range jobs {\n\t\tgo func(j job) {\n\t\t\tdefer wg.Done()\n\t\t\tif err := j.fn(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tmu.Lock()\n\t\t\t\terrs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf(\"%s: %s\", j.name, err.Error()))\n\t\t\t\tmu.Unlock()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}(j)\n\t}\n\twg.Wait()\n\n\tresult := provider.Result{\n\t\tTarget: \"npm:\" + identifier,\n\t}\n\n\tif len(errs) == len(jobs) {\n\t\tresult.Error = strings.Join(errs, \"; \")\n\t\treturn result, nil\n\t}\n\n\tresult.NPM = metrics\n\tif len(errs) > 0 {\n\t\tresult.Error = strings.Join(errs, \"; \")\n\t}\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n\ntype latestResponse struct {\n\tVersion      string            `json:\"version\"`\n\tDependencies map[string]string `json:\"dependencies\"`\n\tLicense      string\n\tRawLicense   json.RawMessage `json:\"license\"`\n}\n\nfunc (p *Provider) fetchLatest(ctx context.Context, pkg string) (*latestResponse, error) {\n\tu := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%s/latest\", p.registryURL, pkg)\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, \"GET\", u, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tresp, err := p.client.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\n\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"npm registry: %d %s\", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))\n\t}\n\n\tvar latest latestResponse\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&latest); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"decoding response: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tlatest.License = parseLicense(latest.RawLicense)\n\treturn &latest, nil\n}\n\nfunc parseLicense(raw json.RawMessage) string {\n\tif len(raw) == 0 {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\n\tvar s string\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &s); err == nil {\n\t\treturn s\n\t}\n\n\tvar obj struct {\n\t\tType string `json:\"type\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &obj); err == nil {\n\t\treturn obj.Type\n\t}\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n\nfunc (p *Provider) fetchLastPublishDays(ctx context.Context, pkg string) (int, error) {\n\tu := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%s\", p.registryURL, pkg)\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, \"GET\", u, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Accept\", \"application/vnd.npm.install-v1+json\")\n\n\tresp, err := p.client.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\n\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"npm registry: %d %s\", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))\n\t}\n\n\tvar meta struct {\n\t\tModified string `json:\"modified\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&meta); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"decoding response: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tt, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, meta.Modified)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt, err = time.Parse(\"2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z\", meta.Modified)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"parsing modified date %q: %w\", meta.Modified, err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tdays := int(math.Floor(time.Since(t).Hours() / 24))\n\tif days < 0 {\n\t\tdays = 0\n\t}\n\treturn days, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *Provider) fetchWeeklyDownloads(ctx context.Context, pkg string) (int, error) {\n\tencodedPkg := pkg\n\tif strings.HasPrefix(pkg, \"@\") {\n\t\tparts := strings.SplitN(pkg, \"/\", 2)\n\t\tif len(parts) == 2 {\n\t\t\tencodedPkg = parts[0] + \"%2F\" + url.PathEscape(parts[1])\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tu := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/downloads/point/last-week/%s\", p.downloadsURL, encodedPkg)\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, \"GET\", u, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\n\tresp, err := p.client.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\n\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"npm downloads API: %d %s\", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))\n\t}\n\n\tvar dl struct {\n\t\tDownloads int `json:\"downloads\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&dl); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"decoding response: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn dl.Downloads, nil\n}\n"
}

     1→package cli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"flag"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"sync"
    10→	"time"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/auth"
    13→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/format"
    14→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"
    15→	ghprovider "github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider/github"
    16→)
    17→
    18→// Version is set at build time via ldflags.
    19→var Version = "dev"
    20→
    21→const timeout = 30 * time.Second
    22→
    23→// Run executes the CLI with the given arguments.
    24→func Run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) error {
    25→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("repiq", flag.ContinueOnError)
    26→	fs.SetOutput(stderr)
    27→
    28→	jsonFlag := fs.Bool("json", false, "output as JSON array (default)")
    29→	ndjsonFlag := fs.Bool("ndjson", false, "output as newline-delimited JSON")
    30→	markdownFlag := fs.Bool("markdown", false, "output as Markdown table")
    31→	versionFlag := fs.Bool("version", false, "print version and exit")
    32→
    33→	fs.Usage = func() {
    34→		_, _ = io.WriteString(stderr, `Usage: repiq [flags] <scheme>:<identifier> [...]
    35→
    36→Fetch objective metrics for OSS libraries and repositories.
    37→
    38→Examples:
    39→  repiq github:facebook/react
    40→  repiq --ndjson github:facebook/react github:vuejs/core
    41→  repiq --markdown github:golang/go
    42→
    43→Flags:
    44→`)
    45→		fs.PrintDefaults()
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
    49→		return err
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	if *versionFlag {
    53→		_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "repiq %s\n", Version)
    54→		return nil
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	targets := fs.Args()
    58→	if len(targets) == 0 {
    59→		fs.Usage()
    60→		return fmt.Errorf("no targets specified")
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	// Determine output format. When multiple flags are set, priority:
    64→	// markdown > ndjson > json (default). This matches the spec's
    65→	// "last specified wins" intent for wrapper scripts that set defaults.
    66→	formatter := format.JSON
    67→	if *ndjsonFlag {
    68→		formatter = format.NDJSON
    69→	}
    70→	if *markdownFlag {
    71→		formatter = format.Markdown
    72→	}
    73→	if *jsonFlag && !*ndjsonFlag && !*markdownFlag {
    74→		formatter = format.JSON
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	// Set up providers.
    78→	resolver := &auth.Resolver{
    79→		Cmd:    auth.ExecRunner{},
    80→		Getenv: os.Getenv,
    81→	}
    82→	token := resolver.ResolveToken()
    83→
    84→	registry := provider.NewRegistry()
    85→	registry.Register(ghprovider.New(token, ""))
    86→
    87→	// Parse and validate all targets first.
    88→	parsed := make([]provider.Target, len(targets))
    89→	for i, raw := range targets {
    90→		t, err := provider.ParseTarget(raw)
    91→		if err != nil {
    92→			return err
    93→		}
    94→		if _, ok := registry.Lookup(t.Scheme); !ok {
    95→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown scheme %q in target %q", t.Scheme, raw)
    96→		}
    97→		parsed[i] = t
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// Fetch all targets in parallel.
   101→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), timeout)
   102→	defer cancel()
   103→
   104→	results := make([]provider.Result, len(parsed))
   105→	var wg sync.WaitGroup
   106→	wg.Add(len(parsed))
   107→	for i, t := range parsed {
   108→		go func(i int, t provider.Target) {
   109→			defer wg.Done()
   110→			p, _ := registry.Lookup(t.Scheme)
   111→			result, err := p.Fetch(ctx, t.Identifier)
   112→			if err != nil {
   113→				results[i] = provider.Result{
   114→					Target: t.Scheme + ":" + t.Identifier,
   115→					Error:  err.Error(),
   116→				}
   117→				return
   118→			}
   119→			results[i] = result
   120→		}(i, t)
   121→	}
   122→	wg.Wait()
   123→
   124→	// Output results.
   125→	if err := formatter(stdout, results); err != nil {
   126→		return fmt.Errorf("formatting output: %w", err)
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	// Exit code: 1 if any result has an error.
   130→	for _, r := range results {
   131→		if r.Error != "" {
   132→			return fmt.Errorf("one or more targets failed")
   133→		}
   134→	}
   135→	return nil
   136→}
   137→
```

     1→package format
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"io"
     7→	"strconv"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// JSON writes results as a JSON array.
    13→func JSON(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {
    14→	if results == nil {
    15→		results = []provider.Result{}
    16→	}
    17→	enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
    18→	enc.SetIndent("", "  ")
    19→	return enc.Encode(results)
    20→}
    21→
    22→// NDJSON writes results as newline-delimited JSON (one object per line).
    23→func NDJSON(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {
    24→	enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
    25→	for _, r := range results {
    26→		if err := enc.Encode(r); err != nil {
    27→			return err
    28→		}
    29→	}
    30→	return nil
    31→}
    32→
    33→// Markdown writes results as a Markdown table grouped by scheme.
    34→func Markdown(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {
    35→	var ghResults []provider.Result
    36→	var errResults []provider.Result
    37→	for _, r := range results {
    38→		if r.GitHub != nil {
    39→			ghResults = append(ghResults, r)
    40→		} else {
    41→			errResults = append(errResults, r)
    42→		}
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	if len(ghResults) > 0 {
    46→		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "| target | stars | forks | open_issues | contributors | release_count | last_commit_days | commits_30d | issues_closed_30d | error |"); err != nil {
    47→			return err
    48→		}
    49→		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"); err != nil {
    50→			return err
    51→		}
    52→		for _, r := range ghResults {
    53→			g := r.GitHub
    54→			if _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, "| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |\n",
    55→				r.Target,
    56→				strconv.Itoa(g.Stars),
    57→				strconv.Itoa(g.Forks),
    58→				strconv.Itoa(g.OpenIssues),
    59→				strconv.Itoa(g.Contributors),
    60→				strconv.Itoa(g.ReleaseCount),
    61→				strconv.Itoa(g.LastCommitDays),
    62→				strconv.Itoa(g.Commits30d),
    63→				strconv.Itoa(g.IssuesClosed30d),
    64→				r.Error,
    65→			); err != nil {
    66→				return err
    67→			}
    68→		}
    69→	}
    70→
    71→	if len(errResults) > 0 {
    72→		if len(ghResults) > 0 {
    73→			if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w); err != nil {
    74→				return err
    75→			}
    76→		}
    77→		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "| target | error |"); err != nil {
    78→			return err
    79→		}
    80→		if _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, "|---|---|"); err != nil {
    81→			return err
    82→		}
    83→		for _, r := range errResults {
    84→			if _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, "| %s | %s |\n", r.Target, r.Error); err != nil {
    85→				return err
    86→			}
    87→		}
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	return nil
    91→}
    92→

     9→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"
    10→)
    11→
    12→func sampleResults() []provider.Result {
    13→	return []provider.Result{
    14→		{
    15→			Target: "github:facebook/react",
    16→			GitHub: &provider.GitHubMetrics{
    17→				Stars:           215000,
    18→				Forks:           45000,
    19→				OpenIssues:      1000,
    20→				Contributors:    1500,
    21→				ReleaseCount:    200,
    22→				LastCommitDays:  1,
    23→				Commits30d:      120,
    24→				IssuesClosed30d: 340,
    25→			},
    26→		},
    27→		{
    28→			Target: "github:nonexistent/repo",
    29→			Error:  "GitHub API: 404 Not Found",
    30→		},
    31→	}
    32→}
    33→
    34→func TestJSON(t *testing.T) {
    35→	var buf bytes.Buffer
    36→	results := sampleResults()
    37→	if err := JSON(&buf, results); err != nil {
    38→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
    39→	}
    40→
    41→	// Should be valid JSON array.
    42→	var parsed []json.RawMessage
    43→	if err := json.Unmarshal(buf.Bytes(), &parsed); err != nil {
    44→		t.Fatalf("invalid JSON: %v\noutput: %s", err, buf.String())
    45→	}
    46→	if len(parsed) != 2 {
    47→		t.Errorf("expected 2 items, got %d", len(parsed))
    48→	}
    49→	// Should end with newline.
    50→	if !strings.HasSuffix(buf.String(), "\n") {
    51→		t.Error("JSON output should end with newline")
    52→	}
    53→}
    54→
    55→func TestNDJSON(t *testing.T) {
    56→	var buf bytes.Buffer
    57→	results := sampleResults()
    58→	if err := NDJSON(&buf, results); err != nil {
    59→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(buf.String()), "\n")
    63→	if len(lines) != 2 {
    64→		t.Errorf("expected 2 lines, got %d", len(lines))
    65→	}
    66→	// Each line should be valid JSON.
    67→	for i, line := range lines {
    68→		var obj json.RawMessage
    69→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &obj); err != nil {
    70→			t.Errorf("line %d is not valid JSON: %v", i, err)
    71→		}
    72→	}
    73→}
    74→
    75→func TestMarkdown(t *testing.T) {
    76→	var buf bytes.Buffer
    77→	results := sampleResults()
    78→	if err := Markdown(&buf, results); err != nil {
    79→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
    80→	}
    81→
    82→	output := buf.String()
    83→	// Should contain table header.
    84→	if !strings.Contains(output, "| target |") {
    85→		t.Error("expected Markdown table header with '| target |'")
    86→	}
    87→	// Should contain separator line.
    88→	if !strings.Contains(output, "|---") {
    89→		t.Error("expected Markdown table separator")
    90→	}
    91→	// Should contain the repo name.
    92→	if !strings.Contains(output, "facebook/react") {
    93→		t.Error("expected facebook/react in output")
    94→	}
    95→	// Error row should show error.
    96→	if !strings.Contains(output, "404 Not Found") {
    97→		t.Error("expected error message in output")
    98→	}
    99→}
   100→

  "old_string": "// Markdown writes results as a Markdown table grouped by scheme.\nfunc Markdown(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {\n\tvar ghResults []provider.Result\n\tvar errResults []provider.Result\n\tfor _, r := range results {\n\t\tif r.GitHub != nil {\n\t\t\tghResults = append(ghResults, r)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\terrResults = append(errResults, r)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif len(ghResults) > 0 {\n\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, \"| target | stars | forks | open_issues | contributors | release_count | last_commit_days | commits_30d | issues_closed_30d | error |\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, \"|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, r := range ghResults {\n\t\t\tg := r.GitHub\n\t\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, \"| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |\\n\",\n\t\t\t\tr.Target,\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.Stars),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.Forks),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.OpenIssues),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.Contributors),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.ReleaseCount),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.LastCommitDays),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.Commits30d),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.IssuesClosed30d),\n\t\t\t\tr.Error,\n\t\t\t); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif len(errResults) > 0 {\n\t\tif len(ghResults) > 0 {\n\t\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, \"| target | error |\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, \"|---|---|\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, r := range errResults {\n\t\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, \"| %s | %s |\\n\", r.Target, r.Error); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// Markdown writes results as a Markdown table grouped by scheme.\nfunc Markdown(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {\n\tvar ghResults []provider.Result\n\tvar npmResults []provider.Result\n\tvar errResults []provider.Result\n\tfor _, r := range results {\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase r.GitHub != nil:\n\t\t\tghResults = append(ghResults, r)\n\t\tcase r.NPM != nil:\n\t\t\tnpmResults = append(npmResults, r)\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\terrResults = append(errResults, r)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tneedSep := false\n\n\tif len(ghResults) > 0 {\n\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, \"| target | stars | forks | open_issues | contributors | release_count | last_commit_days | commits_30d | issues_closed_30d | error |\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, \"|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, r := range ghResults {\n\t\t\tg := r.GitHub\n\t\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, \"| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |\\n\",\n\t\t\t\tr.Target,\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.Stars),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.Forks),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.OpenIssues),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.Contributors),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.ReleaseCount),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.LastCommitDays),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.Commits30d),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(g.IssuesClosed30d),\n\t\t\t\tr.Error,\n\t\t\t); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tneedSep = true\n\t}\n\n\tif len(npmResults) > 0 {\n\t\tif needSep {\n\t\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, \"| target | weekly_downloads | latest_version | last_publish_days | dependencies_count | license | error |\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, \"|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, r := range npmResults {\n\t\t\tn := r.NPM\n\t\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, \"| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |\\n\",\n\t\t\t\tr.Target,\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(n.WeeklyDownloads),\n\t\t\t\tn.LatestVersion,\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(n.LastPublishDays),\n\t\t\t\tstrconv.Itoa(n.DependenciesCount),\n\t\t\t\tn.License,\n\t\t\t\tr.Error,\n\t\t\t); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tneedSep = true\n\t}\n\n\tif len(errResults) > 0 {\n\t\tif needSep {\n\t\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, \"| target | error |\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintln(w, \"|---|---|\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, r := range errResults {\n\t\t\tif _, err := fmt.Fprintf(w, \"| %s | %s |\\n\", r.Target, r.Error); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}"
}

---

# Code Review Report

**Branch:** `feat/6-npm-provider`
**Verdict:** WARNING -- No CRITICAL issues. A few HIGH and MEDIUM items to address.

All tests pass. `go vet` is clean.

---

## File 1: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/6-npm-provider/internal/provider/npm/npm.go`

### [HIGH] Missing input validation on package name -- potential SSRF / URL injection
**File:** `internal/provider/npm/npm.go:136`
**Issue:** The `identifier` (package name) is interpolated directly into URLs via `fmt.Sprintf` without validation. The GitHub provider validates `owner/repo` against a regex (`^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+$`), but the npm provider does no equivalent check. A crafted identifier like `../../some/path` or one containing query parameters (`react?foo=bar`) would be sent as-is to the npm registry.

While npm package names do allow `@scope/name` format, they have a well-defined grammar. Without validation, an attacker-controlled input could manipulate the request URL.

**Fix:** Add a validation function similar to the GitHub provider's `parseIdentifier`:

```go
import "regexp"

var validPkgRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^(@[a-zA-Z0-9][\w.-]*/)?[a-zA-Z0-9][\w.-]*$`)

func validatePackageName(name string) error {
    if !validPkgRe.MatchString(name) {
        return fmt.Errorf("invalid npm package name %q", name)
    }
    return nil
}
```

Then call it at the top of `Fetch` before the parallel jobs.

---

### [HIGH] No `http.Client` timeout configured
**File:** `internal/provider/npm/npm.go:40-41`
**Issue:** The `http.Client` is created with `&http.Client{}` which has no timeout. Although the CLI passes a context with a 30-second timeout, if the npm provider is ever used outside the CLI (as a library), individual HTTP requests could hang indefinitely. The GitHub provider has the same pattern but uses the `go-github` client which has its own timeout handling.

**Fix:**
```go
return &Provider{
    registryURL:  strings.TrimRight(registryURL, "/"),
    downloadsURL: strings.TrimRight(downloadsURL, "/"),
    client:       &http.Client{Timeout: 30 * time.Second},
}
```

---

### [MEDIUM] Unbounded response body size
**File:** `internal/provider/npm/npm.go:153, 201, 251`
**Issue:** `json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(...)` reads the entire response body into memory. A malicious or misconfigured server could return a very large response. The `fetchLastPublishDays` uses `application/vnd.npm.install-v1+json` which is the abbreviated metadata, so that is fine. But `fetchLatest` hits `/package/latest` which is a full version document -- normally small, but not bounded.

**Fix:** Wrap `resp.Body` with `io.LimitReader`:
```go
body := io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 5*1024*1024) // 5 MB limit
if err := json.NewDecoder(body).Decode(&latest); err != nil {
```

This is low-risk in practice since this tool is CLI-only targeting the real npm registry, so this is MEDIUM rather than HIGH.

---

### [LOW] Deslop: Comment on line 221-224 is fine
The comment on `fetchWeeklyDownloads` explaining why scoped packages need special URL encoding is genuinely useful and not restating the code. No slop detected in this file.

**No issues (Deslop):** Comments are concise and justified. No over-abstraction.

---

## File 2: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/6-npm-provider/internal/provider/npm/npm_test.go`

### [MEDIUM] Hardcoded future date in test will cause flaky test over time
**File:** `internal/provider/npm/npm_test.go:43`
**Issue:** The mock server returns `"modified": "2026-02-10T12:00:00.000Z"`. The test on line 103 asserts `n.LastPublishDays >= 0`. This will pass today (2026-02-25), yielding ~15 days. But if the code is ever run before 2026-02-10, the value would be 0 (clamped). More importantly, as time passes, the value keeps growing, which is fine for `>= 0` but makes the test less meaningful.

**Fix:** Consider computing the date dynamically in the test:
```go
modified := time.Now().Add(-15 * 24 * time.Hour).Format("2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z")
```
Then assert `LastPublishDays` is approximately 15 (e.g., between 14 and 16 to account for timing).

---

### No issues (Deslop): Test file is clean. Good coverage of success, 404, empty identifier, scoped packages, legacy license format, zero dependencies, and partial failure. Seven test functions covering all key paths.

---

## File 3: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/6-npm-provider/internal/provider/provider.go`

**No issues.** Clean, minimal interface definition. 38 lines. Structs are well-named with proper JSON tags. `omitempty` used correctly on optional fields.

---

## File 4: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/6-npm-provider/internal/provider/provider_test.go`

**No issues.** Tests are straightforward. The stub provider pattern for interface testing is clean and idiomatic.

---

## File 5: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/6-npm-provider/internal/cli/cli.go`

### [MEDIUM] Format flag precedence logic is subtly confusing
**File:** `internal/cli/cli.go:68-77`
**Issue:** The comment says "markdown > ndjson > json" but the code then has a special case on line 75 that re-asserts JSON only when `--json` is set AND the others are not. This is redundant -- `formatter` is already `format.JSON` by default on line 68. If someone passes `--json --markdown`, markdown wins (correct), but the code path is harder to follow than it needs to be.

**Fix:** Remove the redundant block:
```go
formatter := format.JSON
if *ndjsonFlag {
    formatter = format.NDJSON
}
if *markdownFlag {
    formatter = format.Markdown
}
// Remove lines 75-77 entirely; they are dead code
```

---

### [LOW] No test file for cli.go in the reviewed set
**File:** `internal/cli/cli.go`
**Issue:** While not in scope of this review, the CLI `Run` function has no unit test visible. The function is 60 lines (at the boundary of the 50-line guideline) and does argument parsing + orchestration. Consider testing at least the flag parsing and error paths.

---

**No issues (Deslop):** Comments are minimal and useful. No over-abstraction.

---

## File 6: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/6-npm-provider/internal/format/format.go`

### [MEDIUM] Markdown table content is not escaped
**File:** `internal/format/format.go:60, 92, 120`
**Issue:** If any metric field contains a pipe character `|`, the Markdown table will break. For example, an npm license like `"MIT | Apache-2.0"` or an error message containing `|` would corrupt the table layout.

**Fix:** Add a simple escape helper:
```go
func escapeMarkdown(s string) string {
    return strings.ReplaceAll(s, "|", "\\|")
}
```
Then wrap string fields passed to `fmt.Fprintf`:
```go
fmt.Fprintf(w, "| %s | %s | ... |\n",
    escapeMarkdown(r.Target),
    strconv.Itoa(n.WeeklyDownloads),
    escapeMarkdown(n.LatestVersion),
    // ...
    escapeMarkdown(n.License),
    escapeMarkdown(r.Error),
)
```

---

### [LOW] `Markdown` function is 93 lines
**File:** `internal/format/format.go:34-127`
**Issue:** At 93 lines, this function exceeds the 50-line guideline. However, the structure is inherently repetitive (GitHub table, npm table, error table) and extracting sub-functions would likely make it harder to read, not easier. The repetition is justified by the different column sets per scheme.

**Suggestion:** If more providers are added in the future, consider a table-driven approach. For now, this is acceptable.

---

**No issues (Deslop):** No unnecessary comments. Clean functional style.

---

## File 7: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/.git/wt/feat/6-npm-provider/internal/format/format_test.go`

**No issues.** Good coverage: JSON, NDJSON, Markdown, npm-specific Markdown, mixed results, and empty input edge cases. Tests validate structure (valid JSON, line counts, contains expected strings) rather than brittle exact string matching.

---

### Verdict: WARNING

No CRITICAL issues. Two HIGH issues found (missing input validation on package name, no HTTP client timeout). These are worth fixing before merge but are not blocking given that this is a CLI tool where the user controls the input. The MEDIUM issues (unbounded response body, hardcoded test dates, redundant flag logic, unescaped Markdown) should be addressed but can be merged with caution.

### Priority fixes:
1. **Add npm package name validation** (parity with GitHub provider's `parseIdentifier`)
2. **Set HTTP client timeout** (defense in depth)
3. **Escape pipe characters in Markdown output** (correctness)
4. **Remove dead code in CLI flag precedence** (readability)

     1→package provider
     2→
     3→// Registry maps scheme names to providers.
     4→type Registry struct {
     5→	providers map[string]Provider
     6→}
     7→
     8→// NewRegistry creates an empty registry.
     9→func NewRegistry() *Registry {
    10→	return &Registry{providers: make(map[string]Provider)}
    11→}
    12→
    13→// Register adds a provider to the registry, keyed by its scheme.
    14→func (r *Registry) Register(p Provider) {
    15→	r.providers[p.Scheme()] = p
    16→}
    17→
    18→// Lookup returns the provider for the given scheme.
    19→func (r *Registry) Lookup(scheme string) (Provider, bool) {
    20→	p, ok := r.providers[scheme]
    21→	return p, ok
    22→}
    23→

     1→package format
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"io"
     7→	"strconv"
     8→	"strings"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"
    11→)
    12→
    13→func escapeMarkdown(s string) string {
    14→	return strings.ReplaceAll(s, "|", "\\|")
    15→}
    16→
    17→// JSON writes results as a JSON array.
    18→func JSON(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {
    19→	if results == nil {
    20→		results = []provider.Result{}
    21→	}
    22→	enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
    23→	enc.SetIndent("", "  ")
    24→	return enc.Encode(results)
    25→}
    26→
    27→// NDJSON writes results as newline-delimited JSON (one object per line).
    28→func NDJSON(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {
    29→	enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
    30→	for _, r := range results {
    31→		if err := enc.Encode(r); err != nil {
    32→			return err
    33→		}
    34→	}
    35→	return nil
    36→}
    37→
    38→// Markdown writes results as a Markdown table grouped by scheme.
    39→func Markdown(w io.Writer, results []provider.Result) error {
    40→	var ghResults []provider.Result
    41→	var npmResults []provider.Result
    42→	var errResults []provider.Result
    43→	for _, r := range results {
    44→		switch {
    45→		case r.GitHub != nil:
    46→			ghResults = append(ghResults, r)
    47→		case r.NPM != nil:
    48→			npmResults = append(npmResults, r)
    49→		default:
    50→			errResults = append(errResults, r)
    51→		}
    52→	}
    53→

   131→	return nil

     1→package npm
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"

    15→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"

    17→
    18→var validPkgRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^(@[a-zA-Z0-9][\w.-]*/)?[a-zA-Z0-9][\w.-]*$`)
    19→
    20→const (
    21→	defaultRegistryURL  = "https://registry.npmjs.org"
    22→	defaultDownloadsURL = "https://api.npmjs.org"
    23→)
    24→
    25→// Provider fetches metrics from the npm registry.
    26→type Provider struct {
    27→	registryURL  string
    28→	downloadsURL string
    29→	client       *http.Client
    30→}
    31→
    32→// New creates an npm provider. Pass empty strings for default URLs.
    33→func New(registryURL, downloadsURL string) *Provider {
    34→	if registryURL == "" {
    35→		registryURL = defaultRegistryURL
    36→	}
    37→	if downloadsURL == "" {
    38→		downloadsURL = defaultDownloadsURL
    39→	}
    40→	return &Provider{
    41→		registryURL:  strings.TrimRight(registryURL, "/"),
    42→		downloadsURL: strings.TrimRight(downloadsURL, "/"),
    43→		client:       &http.Client{Timeout: 30 * time.Second},
    44→	}
    45→}
    46→
    47→func (p *Provider) Scheme() string { return "npm" }
    48→
    49→func (p *Provider) Fetch(ctx context.Context, identifier string) (provider.Result, error) {
    50→	if identifier == "" || !validPkgRe.MatchString(identifier) {
    51→		return provider.Result{
    52→			Target: "npm:" + identifier,
    53→			Error:  fmt.Sprintf("invalid npm package name %q", identifier),
    54→		}, nil
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	metrics := &provider.NPMMetrics{}
    58→	var mu sync.Mutex
    59→	var wg sync.WaitGroup
    60→	var errs []string
    61→
    62→	type job struct {
    63→		name string
    64→		fn   func(context.Context) error
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	jobs := []job{
    68→		{"latest", func(ctx context.Context) error {
    69→			latest, err := p.fetchLatest(ctx, identifier)
    70→			if err != nil {
    71→				return err
    72→			}
    73→			mu.Lock()
    74→			metrics.LatestVersion = latest.Version
    75→			metrics.DependenciesCount = len(latest.Dependencies)
    76→			metrics.License = latest.License
    77→			mu.Unlock()
    78→			return nil
    79→		}},
    80→		{"modified", func(ctx context.Context) error {
    81→			days, err := p.fetchLastPublishDays(ctx, identifier)
    82→			if err != nil {
    83→				return err
    84→			}
    85→			mu.Lock()
    86→			metrics.LastPublishDays = days
    87→			mu.Unlock()
    88→			return nil
    89→		}},
    90→		{"downloads", func(ctx context.Context) error {
    91→			count, err := p.fetchWeeklyDownloads(ctx, identifier)
    92→			if err != nil {
    93→				return err
    94→			}
    95→			mu.Lock()
    96→			metrics.WeeklyDownloads = count
    97→			mu.Unlock()
    98→			return nil
    99→		}},
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	wg.Add(len(jobs))
   103→	for _, j := range jobs {
   104→		go func(j job) {
   105→			defer wg.Done()
   106→			if err := j.fn(ctx); err != nil {
   107→				mu.Lock()
   108→				errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s", j.name, err.Error()))
   109→				mu.Unlock()
   110→			}
   111→		}(j)
   112→	}
   113→	wg.Wait()
   114→
   115→	result := provider.Result{
   116→		Target: "npm:" + identifier,
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	if len(errs) == len(jobs) {
   120→		result.Error = strings.Join(errs, "; ")
   121→		return result, nil
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	result.NPM = metrics
   125→	if len(errs) > 0 {
   126→		result.Error = strings.Join(errs, "; ")
   127→	}
   128→	return result, nil
   129→}
   130→
   131→type latestResponse struct {
   132→	Version      string            `json:"version"`
   133→	Dependencies map[string]string `json:"dependencies"`
   134→	License      string
   135→	RawLicense   json.RawMessage `json:"license"`
   136→}
   137→
   138→func (p *Provider) fetchLatest(ctx context.Context, pkg string) (*latestResponse, error) {
   139→	u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/latest", p.registryURL, pkg)
   140→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "GET", u, nil)
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		return nil, err
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	resp, err := p.client.Do(req)
   146→	if err != nil {
   147→		return nil, err
   148→	}
   149→	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
   150→
   151→	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   152→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("npm registry: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	var latest latestResponse
   156→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&latest); err != nil {
   157→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("decoding response: %w", err)
   158→	}
   159→
   160→	latest.License = parseLicense(latest.RawLicense)
   161→	return &latest, nil
   162→}
   163→
   164→func parseLicense(raw json.RawMessage) string {
   165→	if len(raw) == 0 {
   166→		return ""
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	var s string
   170→	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &s); err == nil {
   171→		return s
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	var obj struct {
   175→		Type string `json:"type"`
   176→	}
   177→	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &obj); err == nil {
   178→		return obj.Type
   179→	}
   180→	return ""
   181→}
   182→
   183→func (p *Provider) fetchLastPublishDays(ctx context.Context, pkg string) (int, error) {
   184→	u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s", p.registryURL, pkg)
   185→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "GET", u, nil)
   186→	if err != nil {
   187→		return 0, err
   188→	}
   189→	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/vnd.npm.install-v1+json")
   190→
   191→	resp, err := p.client.Do(req)
   192→	if err != nil {
   193→		return 0, err
   194→	}
   195→	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
   196→
   197→	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   198→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("npm registry: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	var meta struct {
   202→		Modified string `json:"modified"`
   203→	}
   204→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&meta); err != nil {
   205→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("decoding response: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, meta.Modified)
   209→	if err != nil {
   210→		t, err = time.Parse("2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z", meta.Modified)
   211→		if err != nil {
   212→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing modified date %q: %w", meta.Modified, err)
   213→		}
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	days := int(math.Floor(time.Since(t).Hours() / 24))
   217→	if days < 0 {
   218→		days = 0
   219→	}
   220→	return days, nil
   221→}
   222→
   223→func (p *Provider) fetchWeeklyDownloads(ctx context.Context, pkg string) (int, error) {
   224→	// For scoped packages (@scope/name), the npm downloads API accepts
   225→	// the package name with the slash URL-encoded as %2F.
   226→	// We build the URL manually to preserve this encoding.
   227→	encodedPkg := pkg
   228→	if strings.HasPrefix(pkg, "@") {
   229→		parts := strings.SplitN(pkg, "/", 2)
   230→		if len(parts) == 2 {
   231→			encodedPkg = url.PathEscape(parts[0]) + "/" + url.PathEscape(parts[1])
   232→		}
   233→	}
   234→
   235→	u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/downloads/point/last-week/%s", p.downloadsURL, encodedPkg)
   236→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "GET", u, nil)
   237→	if err != nil {
   238→		return 0, err
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	resp, err := p.client.Do(req)
   242→	if err != nil {
   243→		return 0, err
   244→	}
   245→	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
   246→
   247→	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   248→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("npm downloads API: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, http.StatusText(resp.StatusCode))
   249→	}
   250→
   251→	var dl struct {
   252→		Downloads int `json:"downloads"`
   253→	}
   254→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&dl); err != nil {
   255→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("decoding response: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→	return dl.Downloads, nil
   258→}
   259→

     1→package cli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"flag"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"sync"
    10→	"time"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/auth"
    13→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/format"
    14→	"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider"
    15→	ghprovider "github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider/github"
    16→	npmprovider "github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider/npm"
    17→)
    18→
    19→// Version is set at build time via ldflags.
    20→var Version = "dev"
    21→
    22→const timeout = 30 * time.Second
    23→
    24→// Run executes the CLI with the given arguments.
    25→func Run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) error {
    26→	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("repiq", flag.ContinueOnError)
    27→	fs.SetOutput(stderr)
    28→
    29→	jsonFlag := fs.Bool("json", false, "output as JSON array (default)")
    30→	ndjsonFlag := fs.Bool("ndjson", false, "output as newline-delimited JSON")
    31→	markdownFlag := fs.Bool("markdown", false, "output as Markdown table")
    32→	versionFlag := fs.Bool("version", false, "print version and exit")
    33→
    34→	fs.Usage = func() {
    35→		_, _ = io.WriteString(stderr, `Usage: repiq [flags] <scheme>:<identifier> [...]
    36→
    37→Fetch objective metrics for OSS libraries and repositories.
    38→
    39→Examples:
    40→  repiq github:facebook/react
    41→  repiq npm:react
    42→  repiq --ndjson github:facebook/react npm:react
    43→  repiq --markdown github:golang/go
    44→
    45→Flags:
    46→`)
    47→		fs.PrintDefaults()
    48→	}
    49→
    50→	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
    51→		return err
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	if *versionFlag {
    55→		_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "repiq %s\n", Version)
    56→		return nil
    57→	}
    58→
    59→	targets := fs.Args()
    60→	if len(targets) == 0 {
    61→		fs.Usage()
    62→		return fmt.Errorf("no targets specified")
    63→	}
    64→
    65→	// Determine output format. When multiple flags are set, priority:
    66→	// markdown > ndjson > json (default). This matches the spec's
    67→	// "last specified wins" intent for wrapper scripts that set defaults.
    68→	formatter := format.JSON
    69→	if *ndjsonFlag {
    70→		formatter = format.NDJSON
    71→	}
    72→	if *markdownFlag {
    73→		formatter = format.Markdown
    74→	}
    75→	if *jsonFlag && !*ndjsonFlag && !*markdownFlag {
    76→		formatter = format.JSON
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// Set up providers.
    80→	resolver := &auth.Resolver{
    81→		Cmd:    auth.ExecRunner{},
    82→		Getenv: os.Getenv,
    83→	}
    84→	token := resolver.ResolveToken()
    85→
    86→	registry := provider.NewRegistry()
    87→	registry.Register(ghprovider.New(token, ""))
    88→	registry.Register(npmprovider.New("", ""))
    89→
    90→	// Parse and validate all targets first.
    91→	parsed := make([]provider.Target, len(targets))
    92→	for i, raw := range targets {
    93→		t, err := provider.ParseTarget(raw)
    94→		if err != nil {
    95→			return err
    96→		}
    97→		if _, ok := registry.Lookup(t.Scheme); !ok {
    98→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown scheme %q in target %q", t.Scheme, raw)
    99→		}
   100→		parsed[i] = t
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	// Fetch all targets in parallel.
   104→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), timeout)
   105→	defer cancel()
   106→
   107→	results := make([]provider.Result, len(parsed))
   108→	var wg sync.WaitGroup
   109→	wg.Add(len(parsed))
   110→	for i, t := range parsed {
   111→		go func(i int, t provider.Target) {
   112→			defer wg.Done()
   113→			p, _ := registry.Lookup(t.Scheme)
   114→			result, err := p.Fetch(ctx, t.Identifier)
   115→			if err != nil {
   116→				results[i] = provider.Result{
   117→					Target: t.Scheme + ":" + t.Identifier,
   118→					Error:  err.Error(),
   119→				}
   120→				return
   121→			}
   122→			results[i] = result
   123→		}(i, t)
   124→	}
   125→	wg.Wait()
   126→
   127→	// Output results.
   128→	if err := formatter(stdout, results); err != nil {
   129→		return fmt.Errorf("formatting output: %w", err)
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	// Exit code: 1 if any result has an error.
   133→	for _, r := range results {
   134→		if r.Error != "" {
   135→			return fmt.Errorf("one or more targets failed")
   136→		}
   137→	}
   138→	return nil
   139→}
   140→
```

  "content": "# Adding a New Provider\n\nrepiq に新しいプロバイダーを追加する手順。\n\n## 変更が必要なファイル\n\n| ファイル | 変更内容 |\n|----------|---------|\n| `internal/provider/provider.go` | Metrics 構造体の追加、Result にフィールド追加 |\n| `internal/provider/provider_test.go` | 新 Metrics の Result テスト追加 |\n| `internal/provider/<scheme>/<scheme>.go` | プロバイダー実装 (新規) |\n| `internal/provider/<scheme>/<scheme>_test.go` | ユニットテスト (新規) |\n| `internal/cli/cli.go` | プロバイダーの登録 |\n| `internal/format/format.go` | Markdown テーブルの追加 |\n| `internal/format/format_test.go` | テストデータの追加 |\n\n変更不要: `target.go`, `registry.go`, `cmd/repiq/main.go`\n\n## Step 1: Metrics 構造体を定義する\n\n`internal/provider/provider.go` に Metrics 構造体を追加し、`Result` に `omitempty` 付きフィールドを追加する。\n\n```go\n// provider.go\n\ntype CratesMetrics struct {\n    RecentDownloads int    `json:\"recent_downloads\"`\n    LatestVersion   string `json:\"latest_version\"`\n    // ...\n}\n\ntype Result struct {\n    Target string         `json:\"target\"`\n    GitHub *GitHubMetrics `json:\"github,omitempty\"`\n    NPM    *NPMMetrics    `json:\"npm,omitempty\"`\n    Crates *CratesMetrics `json:\"crates,omitempty\"`  // 追加\n    Error  string         `json:\"error,omitempty\"`\n}\n```\n\n`provider_test.go` にテストケースを追加:\n\n```go\nfunc TestResultCratesSuccess(t *testing.T) {\n    r := provider.Result{\n        Target: \"crates:serde\",\n        Crates: &provider.CratesMetrics{RecentDownloads: 1000000},\n    }\n    if r.Crates == nil {\n        t.Fatal(\"expected Crates to be non-nil\")\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Step 2: プロバイダーを実装する\n\n`internal/provider/<scheme>/` ディレクトリを作成し、以下のインターフェースを実装する:\n\n```go\ntype Provider interface {\n    Scheme() string\n    Fetch(ctx context.Context, identifier string) (Result, error)\n}\n```\n\n### 構造\n\n```go\n// internal/provider/crates/crates.go\npackage crates\n\nimport (\n    \"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider\"\n)\n\ntype Provider struct {\n    baseURL string\n    client  *http.Client\n}\n\n// New はテスト用に baseURL を差し替えられるようにする。\n// 空文字を渡すとデフォルト URL を使う。\nfunc New(baseURL string) *Provider {\n    if baseURL == \"\" {\n        baseURL = \"https://crates.io/api/v1\"\n    }\n    return &Provider{\n        baseURL: strings.TrimRight(baseURL, \"/\"),\n        client:  &http.Client{Timeout: 30 * time.Second},\n    }\n}\n\nfunc (p *Provider) Scheme() string { return \"crates\" }\n\nfunc (p *Provider) Fetch(ctx context.Context, identifier string) (provider.Result, error) {\n    // 実装\n}\n```\n\n### 実装ルール\n\n| ルール | 理由 |\n|--------|------|\n| `New()` で baseURL を受け取る | httptest でモックサーバーに差し替え可能にする |\n| `http.Client` に `Timeout: 30s` を設定する | context タイムアウトが未設定でもハングしない |\n| identifier をバリデーションする | SSRF / URL injection 防止 |\n| エラーは `Result.Error` に入れ、Go error は返さない | CLI の一括取得で部分失敗を許容するため |\n| 全失敗時は `Result.Error` のみ設定し Metrics は nil | JSON で `\"crates\": null` にならず、フィールドごと省略される |\n| 部分失敗時は取得できた Metrics を設定しつつ Error も記録 | 利用可能なデータは返す |\n| `resp.Body.Close()` は `defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()` | errcheck lint 対応 |\n\n### 並列取得パターン\n\n複数 API エンドポイントからデータを取得する場合は `sync.WaitGroup` + `sync.Mutex` で並列実行する:\n\n```go\nfunc (p *Provider) Fetch(ctx context.Context, identifier string) (provider.Result, error) {\n    metrics := &provider.CratesMetrics{}\n    var mu sync.Mutex\n    var wg sync.WaitGroup\n    var errs []string\n\n    type job struct {\n        name string\n        fn   func(context.Context) error\n    }\n\n    jobs := []job{\n        {\"info\", func(ctx context.Context) error {\n            // API 呼び出し → mu.Lock() → metrics に書き込み → mu.Unlock()\n        }},\n        {\"downloads\", func(ctx context.Context) error {\n            // ...\n        }},\n    }\n\n    wg.Add(len(jobs))\n    for _, j := range jobs {\n        go func(j job) {\n            defer wg.Done()\n            if err := j.fn(ctx); err != nil {\n                mu.Lock()\n                errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf(\"%s: %s\", j.name, err.Error()))\n                mu.Unlock()\n            }\n        }(j)\n    }\n    wg.Wait()\n\n    result := provider.Result{Target: \"crates:\" + identifier}\n\n    if len(errs) == len(jobs) {\n        // 全失敗\n        result.Error = strings.Join(errs, \"; \")\n        return result, nil\n    }\n    result.Crates = metrics\n    if len(errs) > 0 {\n        // 部分失敗\n        result.Error = strings.Join(errs, \"; \")\n    }\n    return result, nil\n}\n```\n\n## Step 3: テストを書く\n\n`httptest.Server` + `http.NewServeMux()` でモックサーバーを立てる。\n\n```go\n// internal/provider/crates/crates_test.go\npackage crates\n\nfunc setupMockServer(t *testing.T) *httptest.Server {\n    t.Helper()\n    mux := http.NewServeMux()\n\n    mux.HandleFunc(\"GET /crates/serde\", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {\n        json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]any{...})\n    })\n\n    srv := httptest.NewServer(mux)\n    t.Cleanup(srv.Close)\n    return srv\n}\n\nfunc TestFetchSuccess(t *testing.T) {\n    srv := setupMockServer(t)\n    p := New(srv.URL)\n    result, err := p.Fetch(context.Background(), \"serde\")\n    // assertions...\n}\n```\n\n### 必須テストケース\n\n| テスト | 内容 |\n|--------|------|\n| `TestScheme` | `Scheme()` が正しいスキーム名を返す |\n| `TestFetchSuccess` | 正常系で全メトリクスが取得される |\n| `TestFetchNotFound` | 存在しないパッケージで `Result.Error` が設定される |\n| `TestFetchEmptyIdentifier` | 空文字でバリデーションエラー |\n| `TestFetchInvalidIdentifier` | 不正な文字列でバリデーションエラー |\n| `TestFetchPartialFailure` | 一部 API 失敗時に Metrics + Error が両方設定される |\n\nテスト内の日時は `time.Now().Add(-N * 24 * time.Hour)` で動的に生成し、flaky test を防ぐ。\n\n## Step 4: CLI に登録する\n\n`internal/cli/cli.go` に 2 箇所追加:\n\n```go\nimport (\n    // ...\n    cratesprovider \"github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/internal/provider/crates\"\n)\n\n// Run() 内の provider 登録部分:\nregistry.Register(cratesprovider.New(\"\"))\n```\n\nUsage の Examples にも追加:\n\n```\n  repiq crates:serde\n```\n\n## Step 5: Markdown フォーマッターを更新する\n\n`internal/format/format.go` に 3 箇所追加:\n\n### 1. 結果の分類\n\n```go\nvar cratesResults []provider.Result\n\ncase r.Crates != nil:\n    cratesResults = append(cratesResults, r)\n```\n\n### 2. テーブルのレンダリング\n\n```go\nif len(cratesResults) > 0 {\n    if needSep {\n        fmt.Fprintln(w)\n    }\n    fmt.Fprintln(w, \"| target | recent_downloads | latest_version | ... | error |\")\n    fmt.Fprintln(w, \"|---|---|---|---|---|\")\n    for _, r := range cratesResults {\n        c := r.Crates\n        fmt.Fprintf(w, \"| %s | %s | %s | ... | %s |\\n\",\n            escapeMarkdown(r.Target),\n            strconv.Itoa(c.RecentDownloads),\n            escapeMarkdown(c.LatestVersion),\n            escapeMarkdown(r.Error),\n        )\n    }\n    needSep = true\n}\n```\n\n文字列フィールドには必ず `escapeMarkdown()` を適用する。\n\n### 3. テストデータの追加\n\n`format_test.go` の `sampleResults()` に新プロバイダーの成功ケースを追加し、既存テストの item 数を更新する。\n\n## Step 6: 検証\n\n```bash\ngo test ./...\ngolangci-lint run\ngo build ./cmd/repiq && ./repiq crates:serde\n```\n\n## Checklist\n\n- [ ] `provider.go` に Metrics 構造体と Result フィールドを追加\n- [ ] `provider_test.go` に Result テストを追加\n- [ ] `internal/provider/<scheme>/` に実装 + テスト\n- [ ] identifier バリデーションを実装\n- [ ] `http.Client` に Timeout を設定\n- [ ] `resp.Body.Close()` を `defer func() { _ = ... }()` で呼ぶ\n- [ ] httptest で success, 404, empty, invalid, partial failure をテスト\n- [ ] `cli.go` に import + `Register()` を追加\n- [ ] `format.go` に Markdown テーブルを追加 (escapeMarkdown 適用)\n- [ ] `format_test.go` にサンプルデータを追加\n- [ ] `go test ./...` pass\n- [ ] `golangci-lint run` 0 issues\n- [ ] CLAUDE.md の scope リストにスキーム名を追加\n"
}

> AGENT

Codex から P1 レビュー 1 件。scoped package の URL エンコーディングの指摘です。

**指摘内容:** `fetchWeeklyDownloads` で `url.PathEscape(parts[0]) + "/" + url.PathEscape(parts[1])` とすると `%40types/node` のように 2 つのパスセグメントになり、npm downloads API が 404 を返す可能性がある。`@scope%2Fname` のように単一トークンにすべき。

手動テストでは `npm:@types/node` と `npm:@babel/core` が正常に動作していましたが、指摘は正しい — 現在の実装は npm API の寛容なルーティングに依存しており、`%2F` エンコードが仕様上正しい形式です。修正します。

`url.PathEscape("@types/node")` = `"@types%2Fnode"` — `@` はエンコードされず `/` だけが `%2F` になります。デコード後のパスは正しいはずですが、ServeMux のルーティングで `%2F` がどう扱われるか再現テストします。

> AGENT

Go 1.24 の ServeMux は `%2F` をパス区切りの `/` として扱わないことが確認できました。これはテストモック側の問題です。プロダクションコードの `url.PathEscape` は npm API に対して正しいエンコードですが、テストの `HandleFunc` パターンを `%2F` 対応に修正する必要があります。

ワイルドカードパターンで対応します。

{
  "command": "cat > /tmp/muxcheck2.go << 'GOEOF'\npackage main\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"net/http/httptest\"\n\t\"net/url\"\n)\n\nfunc main() {\n\tmux := http.NewServeMux()\n\t\n\t// Use wildcard to match rest of path including %2F encoded segments\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"GET /downloads/point/last-week/{pkg...}\", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"MATCHED pkg=%s rawpath=%s\", r.PathValue(\"pkg\"), r.URL.RawPath)\n\t})\n\n\tsrv := httptest.NewServer(mux)\n\tdefer srv.Close()\n\n\tpkg := \"@types/node\"\n\tencoded := url.PathEscape(pkg)\n\tu := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/downloads/point/last-week/%s\", srv.URL, encoded)\n\tfmt.Printf(\"Request URL: %s\\n\", u)\n\n\tresp, err := http.Get(u)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"Error: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\tbody, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)\n\tfmt.Printf(\"Status: %d\\nBody: %s\\n\", resp.StatusCode, body)\n}\nGOEOF\ngo run /tmp/muxcheck2.go",
  "description": "Test wildcard pattern matching with %2F"
}

```
     1→# repiq
     2→
     3→Fetch objective metrics for OSS repositories. Built for AI agents that need fast, non-reasoning data retrieval for library/repository selection.
     4→
     5→No judgments, no recommendations, no scores -- just numbers.
     6→
     7→## Install
     8→
     9→```
    10→go install github.com/yutakobayashidev/repiq/cmd/repiq@latest
    11→```
    12→
    13→## Usage
    14→
    15→```bash
    16→# Single repo (JSON output by default)
    17→repiq github:facebook/react
    18→
    19→# Multiple repos in NDJSON
    20→repiq --ndjson github:facebook/react github:vuejs/core
    21→
    22→# Markdown table
    23→repiq --markdown github:golang/go
    24→```
    25→
    26→## Supported Providers
    27→
    28→| Provider | Target format           |
    29→| -------- | ----------------------- |
    30→| GitHub   | `github:<owner>/<repo>` |
    31→
    32→## GitHub Metrics
    33→
    34→| Metric              | Description                       |
    35→| ------------------- | --------------------------------- |
    36→| `stars`             | Star count                        |
    37→| `forks`             | Fork count                        |
    38→| `open_issues`       | Open issue count (includes PRs)   |
    39→| `contributors`      | Number of contributors            |
    40→| `release_count`     | Total releases                    |
    41→| `last_commit_days`  | Days since last commit            |
    42→| `commits_30d`       | Commits in the last 30 days       |
    43→| `issues_closed_30d` | Issues closed in the last 30 days |
    44→
    45→## Authentication
    46→
    47→Token resolution follows this priority:
    48→
    49→1. `gh auth token` (GitHub CLI)
    50→2. `GITHUB_TOKEN` environment variable
    51→3. Unauthenticated (lower rate limits)
    52→
    53→## Output Formats
    54→
    55→- `--json` (default) -- Single JSON object or array
    56→- `--ndjson` -- Newline-delimited JSON, one object per line
    57→- `--markdown` -- Markdown table
    58→
    59→## Development
    60→
    61→```bash
    62→# Enter dev environment (requires Nix)
    63→nix develop
    64→
    65→# Run tests
    66→go test ./...
    67→
    68→# Lint
    69→golangci-lint run ./...
    70→```
    71→
```

> AGENT

完了しました。まとめ:

**レビュー対応 (P1: scoped package URL encoding)**
- `fetchWeeklyDownloads` で `url.PathEscape(parts[0]) + "/" + url.PathEscape(parts[1])` → `url.PathEscape(pkg)` に修正
- `url.PathEscape("@types/node")` = `@types%2Fnode` で npm API が期待する単一パストークンになる
- Go 1.24 の ServeMux は `%2F` をパス区切りとしてデコードしないため、テストモックをワイルドカードパターン `{pkg...}` に変更
- レビュースレッド resolved 済み

**README 更新**
- Usage に `npm:react`, `npm:@types/node`, 混合プロバイダーの例を追加
- Supported Providers テーブルに npm を追加
- npm Metrics テーブル (5 メトリクス) を追加
- Authentication セクションに npm は認証不要である旨を追記

title:	npm プロバイダーの追加
state:	CLOSED
author:	yutakobayashidev
labels:	epic
comments:	0
assignees:	
projects:	
milestone:	
number:	6
--
## 概要

npm レジストリからパッケージメトリクスを取得する npm プロバイダーを追加し、repiq をマルチソース対応のデータ取得 CLI へ進化させる。

## 背景・目的

GitHub メトリクス単体では AI エージェントのライブラリ選定に情報が不足する。npm の weekly_downloads や last_publish_days を加えることで実利用状況とメンテナンス状況を把握可能にする。

また、2つ目のプロバイダー実装を通じて Provider インターフェースの拡張性を実証する。

**仮説:**
- GitHub + npm メトリクスの組み合わせで AI の選定精度が向上する
- Provider インターフェースが変更なしで npm を統合でき、後続レジストリ追加の実用性が実証される

## スペック

> [overview.md](docs/specs/npm/overview.md) | [scope.md](docs/specs/npm/scope.md)

## 完了条件

- [ ] `repiq npm:react` で 5 メトリクス (weekly_downloads, latest_version, last_publish_days, dependencies_count, license) が JSON 出力される
- [ ] `repiq npm:@types/node` で scoped package が正常に取得される
- [ ] `repiq github:facebook/react npm:react` で混合ターゲットが一括取得される
- [ ] 存在しないパッケージで適切なエラーメッセージが返る
- [ ] 全出力フォーマット (JSON / NDJSON / Markdown) で npm メトリクスが表示される
- [ ] 単体テストが CI で pass する
- [ ] 単一ターゲット取得が 3 秒以内

## 参考情報・補足

- Spec directory: `docs/specs/npm/`
- Success criteria: メトリクスソース数 1→2、ライブラリ評価観点に利用指標を追加
- Out of scope: バージョン指定、ダウンロードトレンド、npm 認証、キャッシュ
```

```
     1→# repiq
     2→
     3→## What is This
     4→
     5→AIエージェントがOSSライブラリ/リポジトリ選定を行うための、高速・非推論型データ取得CLI。客観的指標のみを取得し、構造化データとして返す。判断・推薦・スコア付けは一切行わない。
     6→
     7→## Who is This For
     8→
     9→- AI エージェント (Claude Code, Cursor, Copilot 等) がライブラリ選定の「思考素材」を得るために使う
    10→- Vibe coding ワークフローに組み込む開発者
    11→- OSS コミュニティ全般
    12→
    13→## Problem
    14→
    15→AIエージェントがライブラリ選定を行うとき、必要な客観データを素早く構造化された形で取得する手段がない。既存ツール (Scorecard, ORT, CHAOSS, gh CLI) はそれぞれ特定領域に特化しており、エージェント向けに統一されたデータレイヤーとして設計されていない。
    16→
    17→## Vision
    18→
    19→repiq は「データ正規化レイヤー」として、複数のソース (GitHub, npm, crates.io, PyPI 等) からの指標を統一スキーマで提供する。6-12ヶ月で主要なパッケージレジストリをカバーし、AI エージェントがリポジトリ/ライブラリ選定に必要な全データを repiq 一つで取得できる状態を目指す。
    20→
    21→## Principles
    22→
    23→- **思考しない**: 推論は AI に委譲。repiq は客観データのみを返す
    24→- **最小主義**: 必要最小限の指標から始め、拡張は後から
    25→- **Agent-first**: JSON/NDJSON 出力、固定スキーマ、deterministic
    26→- **速度優先**: Vibe coding の流れを止めない (3秒以内)
    27→- **役割分離**: `repiq -> データ取得` / `AI -> 推論` / `human -> 最終決定`
    28→
```
